```javascript
// ---------- daily mantra ----------
export function getMantraDone() { return get('mantraDone', {}); }
export function setMantraDone(done) {
  const m = getMantraDone();
  m[dayKey()] = done;
  set('mantraDone', m);
  if (done) bumpStat('mantras');
}
export function isMantraDone() {
  const m = getMantraDone();
  return !!m[dayKey()];
}

// ---------- chanting / mala ----------
export function getChantLog() { return get('chantLog', []); }
export function addChantSession(count, mantra) {
  const log = getChantLog();
  log.push({ date: dayKey(), at: Date.now(), count, mantra });
  set('chantLog', log.slice(-500));
  bumpStat('chanting');
}
```

function drawMantra(box) {
  const done = isMantraDone();
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div class="card" style="border-color:rgba(255,154,107,.35)">
      <div class="card-title">Morning Mantra — हस्तको दर्शनम्</div>
      <div class="mantra-sanskrit" style="font-size:34px;line-height:1.6;text-align:center;margin:14px 0;font-family:serif;">
        कराग्रे वसते लक्ष्मीः करमध्ये सरस्वती।<br>
        कराउपस्थे तु गोविन्दः प्रभाते करदर्शनम्।।
      </div>
      <div class="small muted center mt-8">Karāgre Vasate Lakṣmīḥ · Karamadhyhe Sarasvatī · Karāupasthite Tu Govindaḥ · Prabhāte Karadarśanam</div>
      <div class="center mt-14">
        <button class="btn ${done ? 'secondary' : 'primary'} wide" id="mantra-done">${done ? 'Marked done today ✅' : 'I chanted this morning'}</button>
      </div>
      <div class="small muted center mt-8">${done ? 'Blessed day ahead 🙏' : 'Traditionally chanted upon waking, looking at your palms.'}</div>
    </div>
    
    <div class="card mt-14" style="border-color:rgba(157,123,255,.35)">
      <div class="row between" style="align-items:center">
        <div>
          <div style="font-weight:800">Japa Mala — 108</div>
          <div class="small muted">Om Namaḥ Śivāya · Tap a bead to count</div>
        </div>
        <div class="big" id="mala-count">0</div>
      </div>
      <div class="mala-grid" id="mala-grid"></div>
      <div class="center mt-12">
        <button class="btn primary wide" id="mala-tap">Tap Bead / Next</button>
        <button class="btn secondary wide mt-8" id="mala-reset">Reset Mala</button>
        <button class="btn ${done ? 'secondary' : 'primary'} wide mt-8" id="mala-save">Save Session</button>
      </div>
      <div id="mala-msg" class="center small mt-8"></div>
    </div>
  `;
  
  // ... event listeners
}
```

```javascript
let malaCount = 0;
let malaBeads = [];

function renderMala() {
  const grid = document.querySelector('#mala-grid');
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < 108; i++) {
    const b = el(`<div class="mala-bead${i < malaCount ? ' on' : ''}" data-i="${i}"></div>`);
    grid.appendChild(b);
  }
  document.querySelector('#mala-count').textContent = malaCount;
  document.querySelector('#mala-msg').textContent = malaCount >= 108 ? 'Mala complete! 🕉️' : '';
}
```

```javascript
function drawMantra(box) {
  const done = isMantraDone();
  box.innerHTML = `
    <style>
      .mantra-sanskrit { font-size: 34px; line-height: 1.7; text-align: center; margin: 16px 0; font-family: serif; font-weight: 600; }
      .mala-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(12, 1fr); gap: 7px; margin: 14px 0; }
      .mala-bead { width: 30px; height: 30px; border-radius: 50%; border: 2px solid #cbd5e1; background: #f1f5f9; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-size: 10px; transition: all .15s; }
      .mala-bead.on { background: #4f46e5; border-color: #4f46e5; color: #fff; }
      .mala-bead:active { transform: scale(0.9); }
    </style>
    <div class="card" style="border-color:rgba(255,154,107,.35)">
      <div class="card-title">Morning Mantra — हस्तको दर्शनम्</div>
      <div class="mantra-sanskrit">
        कराग्रे वसते लक्ष्मीः करमध्ये सरस्वती।<br>
        कराउपस्थे तु गोविन्दः प्रभाते करदर्शनम्।।
      </div>
      <div class="small muted center mt-8">Karāgre Vasate Lakṣmīḥ · Karamadhyhe Sarasvatī · Karāupasthite Tu Govindaḥ · Prabhāte Karadarśanam</div>
      <div class="center mt-14">
        <button class="btn ${done ? 'secondary' : 'primary'} wide" id="mantra-done">${done ? 'Marked done today ✅' : 'I chanted this morning'}</button>
      </div>
      <div class="small muted center mt-8">${done ? 'May your day be blessed 🙏' : 'Traditionally chanted upon waking, looking at your palms.'}</div>
    </div>
    
    <div class="card mt-14" style="border-color:rgba(157,123,255,.35)">
      <div class="row between" style="align-items:center; margin-bottom:10px">
        <div>
          <div style="font-weight:800">Japa Mala — 108</div>
          <div class="small muted">Om Namaḥ Śivāya · Tap a bead to count</div>
        </div>
        <div class="big" id="mala-count" style="font-size:32px;font-weight:900">${malaCount}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="mala-grid" id="mala-grid"></div>
      <div class="center mt-12">
        <button class="btn primary wide" id="mala-tap">Tap Bead / Next</button>
        <button class="btn secondary wide mt-8" id="mala-reset">Reset Mala</button>
        <button class="btn ${malaCount >= 108 ? 'primary' : 'secondary'} wide mt-8" id="mala-save">${malaCount >= 108 ? 'Save 108 Session' : 'Save Session'}</button>
      </div>
      <div id="mala-msg" class="center small mt-8" style="color:var(--mind-2,#7c3aed);font-weight:700">${malaCount >= 108 ? 'Mala complete! 🕉️' : ''}</div>
    </div>
  `;
  
  renderMalaGrid();
  
  box.querySelector('#mantra-done')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    setMantraDone(true);
    toast('Mantra marked complete for today 🕉️');
    drawMantra(box); // refresh
  });
  
  box.querySelector('#mala-tap')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (malaCount >= 108) return;
    malaCount++;
    beep(600, 0.05, 0.1);
    renderMalaGrid();
    if (malaCount >= 108) {
      chime();
      toast('108 chants complete! 🕉️');
    }
  });
  
  box.querySelector('#mala-reset')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    malaCount = 0;
    renderMalaGrid();
  });
  
  box.querySelector('#mala-save')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (malaCount === 0) { toast('Nothing to save yet'); return; }
    addChantSession(malaCount, 'Om Namaḥ Śivāya');
    toast(`Saved ${malaCount} chants`);
    malaCount = 0;
    renderMalaGrid();
  });
  
  // tap beads directly
  box.querySelectorAll('.mala-bead').forEach(b => {
    b.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const i = +b.dataset.i;
      if (i === malaCount && malaCount < 108) {
        malaCount++;
        beep(600, 0.05, 0.1);
        renderMalaGrid();
        if (malaCount >= 108) { chime(); toast('108 chants complete! 🕉️'); }
      }
    });
  });
}

function renderMalaGrid() {
  const grid = document.querySelector('#mala-grid');
  if (!grid) return;
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < 108; i++) {
    const b = el(`<div class="mala-bead${i < malaCount ? ' on' : ''}" data-i="${i}"></div>`);
    grid.appendChild(b);
  }
  document.querySelector('#mala-count').textContent = malaCount;
  document.querySelector('#mala-msg').textContent = malaCount >= 108 ? 'Mala complete! 🕉️' : '';
  document.querySelector('#mala-save')?.classList.toggle('primary', malaCount >= 108);
  document.querySelector('#mala-save')?.classList.toggle('secondary', malaCount < 108);
}
```

```
// Tiny localStorage wrapper + progress math. No database — everything lives on-device.

const KEY = k => `bb.${k}`;

export function get(k, fallback) {
  try {
    const v = localStorage.getItem(KEY(k));
    return v === null ? fallback : JSON.parse(v);
  } catch { return fallback; }
}
export function set(k, v) { localStorage.setItem(KEY(k), JSON.stringify(v)); }
export function del(k) { localStorage.removeItem(KEY(k)); }

export const dayKey = (d = new Date()) =>
  `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;

// ---------- daily log ----------
export function getLog() { return get('log', {}); }
export function todayLog() {
  const log = getLog();
  const k = dayKey();
  if (!log[k]) log[k] = { min: 0, cal: 0, sessions: 0, kinds: {} };
  return log[k];
}
export function addSession(kind, minutes, kcal) {
  const log = getLog();
  const k = dayKey();
  const day = log[k] || { min: 0, cal: 0, sessions: 0, kinds: {} };
  day.min = +(day.min + minutes).toFixed(1);
  day.cal = Math.round(day.cal + kcal);
  day.sessions += 1;
  day.kinds[kind] = (day.kinds[kind] || 0) + 1;
  log[k] = day;
  set('log', log);
}

// ---------- cumulative stats ----------
export function getStats() {
  return get('stats', { workouts: 0, meditations: 0, dances: 0, gratitude: 0, waterPerfect: 0, kcal: 0, min: 0 });
}
export function bumpStat(key, n = 1) {
  const s = getStats();
  s[key] = (s[key] || 0) + n;
  set('stats', s);
}

// ---------- XP / level ----------
import { LEVELS } from '../data/content.js';
export function getXP() { return get('xp', 0); }
export function addXP(n) { set('xp', getXP() + n); }
export function levelInfo(xp = getXP()) {
  let li = 0;
  LEVELS.forEach((l, i) => { if (xp >= l.at) li = i; });
  const cur = LEVELS[li];
  const next = LEVELS[li + 1];
  const pct = next ? Math.min(100, Math.round(((xp - cur.at) / (next.at - cur.at)) * 100)) : 100;
  return { index: li, name: cur.name, xp, next: next?.at ?? null, pct };
}

// ---------- streak ----------
export function getStreak() {
  const log = getLog();
  let streak = 0;
  const d = new Date();
  // today counts if logged; otherwise streak can still be alive from yesterday
  if (!isActive(log[dayKey(d)])) d.setDate(d.getDate() - 1);
  while (isActive(log[dayKey(d)])) {
    streak++;
    d.setDate(d.getDate() - 1);
  }
  return streak;
}
const isActive = day => day && (day.sessions > 0 || day.min > 0);

// ---------- settings ----------
export const DEFAULT_SETTINGS = {
  name: '', theme: 'dark', gender: 'male', voice: true, sfx: true,
  keepAwake: true, waterRemind: false, haptics: true,
};
export function getSettings() { return { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, ...get('settings', {}) }; }
export function saveSettings(patch) { set('settings', { ...getSettings(), ...patch }); }

// ---------- water ----------
export function getWater(k = dayKey()) { return get('water', {})[k] || 0; }
export function setWater(n, k = dayKey()) {
  const w = get('water', {});
  const prev = w[k] || 0;
  w[k] = Math.max(0, Math.min(12, n));
  if (prev < 8 && w[k] >= 8) bumpStat('waterPerfect');
  set('water', w);
}

// ---------- gratitude ----------
export function getGratitude() { return get('gratitude', []); }
export function addGratitude(text) {
  const g = getGratitude();
  g.unshift({ date: dayKey(), text, at: Date.now() });
  set('gratitude', g.slice(0, 200));
  bumpStat('gratitude');
}

// ---------- challenges ----------
export function getChallengeState() {
  return get('challenge', {}); // { '2026-11-04': {done: true, xp: 30} }
}
export function completeChallenge(xp) {
  const st = getChallengeState();
  const k = dayKey();
  if (st[k]?.done) return false;
  st[k] = { done: true, xp };
  set('challenge', st);
  addXP(xp);
  return true;
}

// ---------- export ----------
export function exportData() {
  const dump = {};
  ['settings', 'log', 'stats', 'xp', 'water', 'gratitude', 'challenge'].forEach(k => { dump[k] = get(k); });
  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(dump, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download = `biggerbody-backup-${dayKey()}.json`;
  a.click();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 2000);
}

export function resetAll() {
  ['settings', 'log', 'stats', 'xp', 'water', 'gratitude', 'challenge', 'quote'].forEach(del);
}
```

    malaCount = 0;

      beep(600, 0.05, 0.1);
      renderMalaGrid();
      if (malaCount >= 108) { chime(); toast('108 chants complete! 🕉️'); }
    }
  });
}

// Tiny localStorage wrapper + progress math. No database — everything lives on-device.

const KEY = k => `bb.${k}`;

export function get(k, fallback) {
  try {
    const v = localStorage.getItem(KEY(k));
    return v === null ? fallback : JSON.parse(v);
  } catch { return fallback; }
}
export function set(k, v) { localStorage.setItem(KEY(k), JSON.stringify(v)); }
export function del(k) { localStorage.removeItem(KEY(k)); }

export const dayKey = (d = new Date()) =>
  `${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;

// ---------- daily log ----------
export function getLog() { return get('log', {}); }
export function todayLog() {
  const log = getLog();
  const k = dayKey();
  if (!log[k]) log[k] = { min: 0, cal: 0, sessions: 0, kinds: {} };
  return log[k];
}
export function addSession(kind, minutes, kcal) {
  const log = getLog();
  const k = dayKey();
  const day = log[k] || { min: 0, cal: 0, sessions: 0, kinds: {} };
  day.min = +(day.min + minutes).toFixed(1);
  day.cal = Math.round(day.cal + kcal);
  day.sessions += 1;
  day.kinds[kind] = (day.kinds[kind] || 0) + 1;
  log[k] = day;
  set('log', log);
}

// ---------- cumulative stats ----------
export function getStats() {
  return get('stats', { workouts: 0, meditations: 0, dances: 0, gratitude: 0, waterPerfect: 0, kcal: 0, min: 0 });
}
export function bumpStat(key, n = 1) {
  const s = getStats();
  s[key] = (s[key] || 0) + n;
  set('stats', s);
}

// ---------- XP / level ----------
import { LEVELS } from '../data/content.js';
export function getXP() { return get('xp', 0); }
export function addXP(n) { set('xp', getXP() + n); }
export function levelInfo(xp = getXP()) {
  let li = 0;
  LEVELS.forEach((l, i) => { if (xp >= l.at) li = i; });
  const cur = LEVELS[li];
  const next = LEVELS[li + 1];
  const pct = next ? Math.min(100, Math.round(((xp - cur.at) / (next.at - cur.at)) * 100)) : 100;
  return { index: li, name: cur.name, xp, next: next?.at ?? null, pct };
}

// ---------- streak ----------
export function getStreak() {
  const log = getLog();
  let streak = 0;
  const d = new Date();
  // today counts if logged; otherwise streak can still be alive from yesterday
  if (!isActive(log[dayKey(d)])) d.setDate(d.getDate() - 1);
  while (isActive(log[dayKey(d)])) {
    streak++;
    d.setDate(d.getDate() - 1);
  }
  return streak;
}
const isActive = day => day && (day.sessions > 0 || day.min > 0);

// ---------- settings ----------
export const DEFAULT_SETTINGS = {
  name: '', theme: 'dark', gender: 'male', voice: true, sfx: true,
  keepAwake: true, waterRemind: false, haptics: true,
};
export function getSettings() { return { ...DEFAULT_SETTINGS, ...get('settings', {}) }; }
export function saveSettings(patch) { set('settings', { ...getSettings(), ...patch }); }

// ---------- water ----------
export function getWater(k = dayKey()) { return get('water', {})[k] || 0; }
export function setWater(n, k = dayKey()) {
  const w = get('water', {});
  const prev = w[k] || 0;
  w[k] = Math.max(0, Math.min(12, n));
  if (prev < 8 && w[k] >= 8) bumpStat('waterPerfect');
  set('water', w);
}

// ---------- gratitude ----------

export function addGratitude(text) {
  const g = getGratitude();
  g.unshift({ date: dayKey(), text, at: Date.now() });

  const m = getMantraDone();
  m[dayKey()] = done;

export function isMantraDone() {

  return !!m[dayKey()];
}

// ---------- chanting / mala ----------
export function getChantLog() { return get('chantLog', []); }
export function addChantSession(count, mantra) {
  const log = getChantLog();
  log.push({ date: dayKey(), at: Date.now(), count, mantra });
  set('chantLog', log.slice(-500));
  bumpStat('chanting');
}

// ---------- challenges ----------
export function getChallengeState() {
  return get('challenge', {}); // { '2026-11-04': {done: true, xp: 30} }
}
export function completeChallenge(xp) {
  const st = getChallengeState();
  const k = dayKey();
  if (st[k]?.done) return false;
  st[k] = { done: true, xp };
  set('challenge', st);
  addXP(xp);
  return true;
}

// ---------- export ----------
export function exportData() {
  const dump = {};
  ['settings', 'log', 'stats', 'xp', 'water', 'gratitude', 'challenge', 'mantraDone', 'chantLog'].forEach(k => { dump[k] = get(k); });
  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(dump, null, 2)], { type: 'application/json' });
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download = `biggerbody-backup-${dayKey()}.json`;
  a.click();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 2000);
}

export function resetAll() {
  ['settings', 'log', 'stats', 'xp', 'water', 'gratitude', 'challenge', 'quote', 'mantraDone', 'chantLog'].forEach(del);
}

src/views/mind.js
```
// Mind Studio — meditation, breathwork, sleep sounds, SOS calm.
import { el, icon, toast } from '../ui/dom.js';
import { MEDITATIONS, SOUNDS, BREATH_PATTERNS, SOS_STEPS } from '../data/content.js';
import { playAmbient, stopAmbient, chime, beep } from '../modules/audio.js';
import { addSession, addXP, bumpStat, getSettings, isMantraDone, setMantraDone, addChantSession } from '../modules/storage.js';
import { playWorkout } from '../modules/player.js';
import { bySection } from '../data/exercises.js';

let tab = 'meditate';
let session = null; // active meditation/breath timer state
let malaCount = 0;

export function render(view) {
  view.innerHTML = `
    <div class="header"><div class="brand">${icon('mind')} Mind Studio</div></div>
    <div class="chips" id="mtabs">
      ${[['meditate', '🧘 Meditate'], ['breathe', '🌬️ Breathe'], ['sleep', '🌙 Sleep Sounds'], ['sos', '🆘 SOS Calm'], ['mantra', '🕉️ Mantra']]
        .map(([id, l]) => `<button class="chip ${tab === id ? 'on' : ''}" data-t="${id}">${l}</button>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div id="mtab"></div>
  `;
  view.querySelector('#mtabs').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const b = e.target.closest('[data-t]');
    if (!b) return;
    stopSession();
    tab = b.dataset.t;
    view.querySelectorAll('#mtabs .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.toggle('on', c === b));
    drawTab(view);
  });
  drawTab(view);
}

function drawTab(view) {
  const box = view.querySelector('#mtab');
  box.innerHTML = '';
  if (tab === 'meditate') drawMeditate(box);
  if (tab === 'breathe') drawBreathe(box);
  if (tab === 'sleep') drawSleep(box);
  if (tab === 'sos') drawSOS(box);
  if (tab === 'mantra') drawMantra(box);
}

/* ---------------- Meditate ---------------- */
function drawMeditate(box) {
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div id="med-player" class="card" style="display:none">
      <div class="center">
        <div class="breath-stage"><div class="breath-circle" id="med-circle">Ready</div></div>
        <div class="big" id="med-name"></div>
        <div class="small muted" id="med-sound"></div>
        <div class="ring mt-14" style="width:150px;height:150px">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 120 120"><circle class="track" cx="60" cy="60" r="52"/>
          <circle class="prog" id="med-prog" cx="60" cy="60" r="52" stroke-dasharray="326.7" stroke-dashoffset="326.7"/></svg>
          <div class="time"><b id="med-time"></b></div>
        </div>
        <div class="small muted mt-8" id="med-line"></div>
        <button class="btn danger wide mt-14" id="med-stop">End session</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="stack mt-14" id="med-list">
      ${MEDITATIONS.map(m => `
        <div class="card tap" data-med="${m.id}">
          <div class="row">
            <div class="e" style="font-size:30px">${m.e}</div>
            <div><div style="font-weight:800">${m.name}</div><div class="small muted">${m.mins} min · ${SOUNDS.find(s => s.id === m.sound)?.name}</div></div>
            <span class="go" style="margin-left:auto">${icon('play')}</span>
          </div>
        </div>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="card mt-14" style="border-color:rgba(157,123,255,.35)">
      <div class="card-title">Gentle movement for heavy days</div>
      <div class="small muted mb-8">When depression makes everything heavy, start tiny. These take under 10 minutes.</div>
      <button class="btn wide" id="med-move">🚶 Try the Heavy Day Lifter</button>
    </div>
    <div class="small muted center mt-14">BiggerBody supports your wellbeing, but it is not medical care. If you are struggling, please reach out to someone you trust or a local professional. You matter. 💛</div>
  `;

  box.querySelector('#med-list').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const card = e.target.closest('[data-med]');
    if (card) startMeditation(MEDITATIONS.find(m => m.id === card.dataset.med));
  });
  box.querySelector('#med-move').addEventListener('click', () => {
    playWorkout('Heavy Day Lifter', bySection('mind'));
  });
  box.querySelector('#med-stop').addEventListener('click', () => stopSession());
}

function startMeditation(m) {
  stopSession();
  const player = document.querySelector('#med-player');
  const list = document.querySelector('#med-list');
  if (!player) return;
  player.style.display = 'block';
  list.style.display = 'none';
  document.querySelector('#med-name').textContent = `${m.e} ${m.name}`;
  document.querySelector('#med-sound').textContent = `Sound: ${SOUNDS.find(s => s.id === m.sound)?.name}`;
  playAmbient(m.sound, 0.5);

  const total = m.mins * 60;
  let left = total, step = 0;
  const circle = document.querySelector('#med-circle');
  const lineEl = document.querySelector('#med-line');
  const timeEl = document.querySelector('#med-time');
  const prog = document.querySelector('#med-prog');
  lineEl.textContent = m.script[0];

  let phaseT = 0;
  session = setInterval(() => {
    left--;
    timeEl.textContent = `${Math.floor(left / 60)}:${String(left % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    prog.style.strokeDashoffset = `${326.7 * (left / total)}`;
    // breathing guide: 4s in, 4s out
    phaseT = (phaseT + 1) % 8;
    if (phaseT < 4) { circle.classList.add('grow'); circle.classList.remove('shrink'); circle.textContent = 'Breathe in'; }
    else { circle.classList.add('shrink'); circle.classList.remove('grow'); circle.textContent = 'Breathe out'; }
    const stepIdx = Math.min(m.script.length - 1, Math.floor(((total - left) / total) * m.script.length));
    if (stepIdx !== step) { step = stepIdx; lineEl.textContent = m.script[step]; beep(740, .1, .08); }
    if (left <= 0) {
      chime();
      addSession('mind', m.mins, 0);
      bumpStat('meditations');
      addXP(30);
      toast('Meditation complete · +30 XP 🧘');
      stopSession();
    }
  }, 1000);
  session.left = left;
}

export function stopSession() {
  if (session) { clearInterval(session); session = null; }
  stopAmbient();
  const player = document.querySelector('#med-player');
  const list = document.querySelector('#med-list');
  if (player) player.style.display = 'none';
  if (list) list.style.display = '';
}

/* ---------------- Breathe ---------------- */
function drawBreathe(box) {
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div class="stack" id="bp-list">
      ${BREATH_PATTERNS.map(p => `
        <div class="card tap" data-bp="${p.id}">
          <div style="font-weight:800">${p.name}</div>
          <div class="small muted mt-8">${p.d}</div>
          <div class="small muted">${p.phases.map(x => x.join('s ')).join(' · ')}</div>
        </div>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div id="bp-player" class="card mt-14" style="display:none">
      <div class="breath-stage"><div class="breath-circle" id="bp-circle">Ready</div></div>
      <div class="center big" id="bp-name"></div>
      <div class="center small muted" id="bp-count">cycles: 0</div>
      <button class="btn danger wide mt-14" id="bp-stop">Stop</button>
    </div>
  `;
  box.querySelector('#bp-list').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const card = e.target.closest('[data-bp]');
    if (!card) return;
    const p = BREATH_PATTERNS.find(x => x.id === card.dataset.bp);
    startBreath(p);
  });
  box.querySelector('#bp-stop')?.addEventListener('click', () => stopSession());
}

function startBreath(p) {
  stopSession();
  document.querySelector('#bp-list').style.display = 'none';
  const player = document.querySelector('#bp-player');
  player.style.display = 'block';
  document.querySelector('#bp-name').textContent = p.name;
  const circle = document.querySelector('#bp-circle');
  const countEl = document.querySelector('#bp-count');
  let cycles = 0, pi = 0, left = p.phases[0][1];
  const setUI = () => {
    const [label, secs] = p.phases[pi];
    circle.textContent = `${label} · ${left}`;
    circle.classList.toggle('grow', label.includes('in'));
    circle.classList.toggle('shrink', label.includes('out'));
  };
  setUI();
  session = setInterval(() => {
    left--;
    if (left <= 0) {
      pi = (pi + 1) % p.phases.length;
      if (pi === 0) { cycles++; countEl.textContent = `cycles: ${cycles}`; beep(660, .08, .08); }
      left = p.phases[pi][1];
      beep(pi === 0 ? 740 : 520, .1, .1);
    }
    setUI();
  }, 1000);
}

/* ---------------- Sleep ---------------- */
function drawSleep(box) {
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div class="stack" id="sound-list">
      ${SOUNDS.map(s => `
        <div class="sound-btn" data-sound="${s.id}">
          <div class="e">${s.e}</div>
          <div><div class="t">${s.name}</div><div class="d">${s.d}</div></div>
          <div class="eq"><i></i><i></i><i></i></div>
        </div>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="card mt-14">
      <div class="card-title">Sleep timer</div>
      <div class="chips" id="sleep-timer">
        ${[15, 30, 60].map(m => `<button class="chip" data-min="${m}">${m} min</button>`).join('')}
        <button class="chip on" data-min="0">No limit</button>
      </div>
      <div class="small muted mt-8">Sound fades out gently when the timer ends.</div>
    </div>
    <div class="small muted center mt-14">Every sound is generated live on your device — infinite, free and offline. 🎧</div>
  `;
  let sleepTimeout = null;
  box.querySelector('#sound-list').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const btn = e.target.closest('[data-sound]');
    if (!btn) return;
    const id = btn.dataset.sound;
    const wasOn = btn.classList.contains('on');
    box.querySelectorAll('.sound-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('on'));
    stopAmbient();
    if (!wasOn) {
      btn.classList.add('on');
      playAmbient(id, 0.55);
    }
  });
  box.querySelector('#sleep-timer').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const b = e.target.closest('[data-min]');
    if (!b) return;
    box.querySelectorAll('#sleep-timer .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.toggle('on', c === b));
    clearTimeout(sleepTimeout);
    const min = +b.dataset.min;
    if (min > 0) {
      sleepTimeout = setTimeout(() => { stopAmbient(); toast('Sleep timer ended — sweet dreams 🌙'); }, min * 60000);
      toast(`Sound stops in ${min} minutes`);
    }
  });
}

/* ---------------- SOS ---------------- */
function drawSOS(box) {
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div class="card" style="border-color:rgba(157,123,255,.4)">
      <div style="font-weight:800;font-size:17px">🆘 Panic or anxiety right now?</div>
      <div class="small muted mt-8">You are safe. Follow the steps below slowly. Nothing else matters for the next minute.</div>
      <button class="btn primary wide mt-14" id="sos-start">Start 60-second calm</button>
    </div>
    <div class="card mt-14">
      <div class="card-title">The 5-4-3-2-1 grounding</div>
      ${SOS_STEPS.map((s, i) => `<div class="row mt-8" style="align-items:flex-start"><b style="color:var(--mind-2)">${i + 1}.</b><span class="small">${s}</span></div>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="card mt-14" style="border-color:rgba(255,154,107,.35)">
      <div class="card-title">For heavy days 💛</div>
      <div class="small">Low mood lies to you. It says "stay down, stay alone." Movement — even 2 minutes — is the kindest counterargument. Try the Heavy Day Lifter above, text a friend, or step into sunlight for 5 minutes.</div>
    </div>
    <div id="sos-player" class="card mt-14" style="display:none">
      <div class="breath-stage"><div class="breath-circle" id="sos-circle">Ready</div></div>
      <div class="center big" id="sos-step"></div>
      <div class="center small muted" id="sos-timer"></div>
      <button class="btn danger wide mt-14" id="sos-stop">I'm okay — stop</button>
    </div>
  `;
  box.querySelector('#sos-start').addEventListener('click', () => {
    stopSession();
    document.querySelector('#sos-player').style.display = 'block';
    playAmbient('ocean', 0.4);
    let step = 0, left = 8;
    const stepEl = document.querySelector('#sos-step');
    const timerEl = document.querySelector('#sos-timer');
    const circle = document.querySelector('#sos-circle');
    const show = () => {
      stepEl.textContent = SOS_STEPS[step] || 'Breathe with the circle.';
      circle.textContent = 'Breathe';
    };
    show();
    session = setInterval(() => {
      left--;
      timerEl.textContent = `${left}s`;
      circle.classList.toggle('grow', left % 8 < 4);
      circle.classList.toggle('shrink', left % 8 >= 4);
      if (left <= 0) {
        step++;
        left = 8;
        beep(600, .12, .1);
        if (step >= SOS_STEPS.length) {
          chime();
          toast('Well done. You handled that. 💛');
          stopSession();
          return;
        }
        show();
      }
    }, 1000);
  });
  box.querySelector('#sos-stop').addEventListener('click', () => stopSession());
}

/* ---------------- Mantra ---------------- */
function drawMantra(box) {
  const done = isMantraDone();
  box.innerHTML = `
    <style>
      .mantra-sanskrit { font-size: 34px; line-height: 1.75; text-align: center; margin: 16px 0; font-family: serif; font-weight: 700; }
      .mala-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(12, 1fr); gap: 7px; margin: 14px 0; }
      .mala-bead { width: 30px; height: 30px; border-radius: 50%; border: 2px solid #cbd5e1; background: #f1f5f9; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-size: 10px; transition: all .15s; }
      .mala-bead.on { background: #4f46e5; border-color: #4f46e5; color: #fff; }
      .mala-bead:active { transform: scale(0.9); }
    </style>
    <div class="card" style="border-color:rgba(255,154,107,.35)">
      <div class="card-title">Morning Mantra — हस्तको दर्शनम्</div>
      <div class="mantra-sanskrit">
        कराग्रे वसते लक्ष्मीः करमध्ये सरस्वती।<br>
        कराउपस्थे तु गोविन्दः प्रभाते करदर्शनम्।।
      </div>
      <div class="small muted center mt-8">Karāgre Vasate Lakṣmīḥ · Karamadhyhe Sarasvatī · Karāupasthite Tu Govindaḥ · Prabhāte Karadarśanam</div>
      <div class="center mt-14">
        <button class="btn ${done ? 'secondary' : 'primary'} wide" id="mantra-done">${done ? 'Marked done today ✅' : 'I chanted this morning'}</button>
      </div>
      <div class="small muted center mt-8">${done ? 'May your day be blessed 🙏' : 'Traditionally chanted upon waking, looking at your palms.'}</div>
    </div>
    
    <div class="card mt-14" style="border-color:rgba(157,123,255,.35)">
      <div class="row between" style="align-items:center; margin-bottom:10px">
        <div>
          <div style="font-weight:800">Japa Mala — 108</div>
          <div class="small muted">Om Namaḥ Śivāya · Tap a bead to count</div>
        </div>
        <div class="big" id="mala-count" style="font-size:32px;font-weight:900">${malaCount}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="mala-grid" id="mala-grid"></div>
      <div class="center mt-12">
        <button class="btn primary wide" id="mala-tap">Tap Bead / Next</button>
        <button class="btn secondary wide mt-8" id="mala-reset">Reset Mala</button>
        <button class="btn ${malaCount >= 108 ? 'primary' : 'secondary'} wide mt-8" id="mala-save">${malaCount >= 108 ? 'Save 108 Session' : 'Save Session'}</button>
      </div>
      <div id="mala-msg" class="center small mt-8" style="color:var(--mind-2,#7c3aed);font-weight:700">${malaCount >= 108 ? 'Mala complete! 🕉️' : ''}</div>
    </div>
  `;

  renderMalaGrid();

  box.querySelector('#mantra-done')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    setMantraDone(true);
    toast('Mantra marked complete for today 🕉️');
    drawMantra(box);
  });

  box.querySelector('#mala-tap')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (malaCount >= 108) return;
    malaCount++;
    beep(600, 0.05, 0.1);
    renderMalaGrid();
    if (malaCount >= 108) { chime(); toast('108 chants complete! 🕉️'); }
  });

  box.querySelector('#mala-reset')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    malaCount = 0;
    renderMalaGrid();
  });

  box.querySelector('#mala-save')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (malaCount === 0) { toast('Nothing to save yet'); return; }
    addChantSession(malaCount, 'Om Namaḥ Śivāya');
    toast(`Saved ${malaCount} chants`);
    malaCount = 0;
    renderMalaGrid();
  });

  box.querySelector('#mala-grid')?.addEventListener('click', e => {
    const bead = e.target.closest('.mala-bead');
    if (!bead) return;
    const i = +bead.dataset.i;
    if (i === malaCount && malaCount < 108) {
      malaCount++;
      beep(600, 0.05, 0.1);
      renderMalaGrid();
      if (malaCount >= 108) { chime(); toast('108 chants complete! 🕉️'); }
    }
  });
}

function renderMalaGrid() {
  const grid = document.querySelector('#mala-grid');
  if (!grid) return;
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < 108; i++) {
    const b = el(`<div class="mala-bead${i < malaCount ? ' on' : ''}" data-i="${i}"></div>`);
    grid.appendChild(b);
  }
  const countEl = document.querySelector('#mala-count');
  const msgEl = document.querySelector('#mala-msg');
  const saveBtn = document.querySelector('#mala-save');
  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = malaCount;
  if (msgEl) msgEl.textContent = malaCount >= 108 ? 'Mala complete! 🕉️' : '';
  if (saveBtn) {
    saveBtn.classList.toggle('primary', malaCount >= 108);
    saveBtn.classList.toggle('secondary', malaCount < 108);
  }
}
```

// Mind Studio — meditation, breathwork, sleep sounds, SOS calm.
import { el, icon, toast } from '../ui/dom.js';
import { MEDITATIONS, SOUNDS, BREATH_PATTERNS, SOS_STEPS } from '../data/content.js';
import { playAmbient, stopAmbient, chime, beep } from '../modules/audio.js';
import { addSession, addXP, bumpStat, getSettings, isMantraDone, setMantraDone, addChantSession } from '../modules/storage.js';
import { playWorkout } from '../modules/player.js';
import { bySection } from '../data/exercises.js';

let tab = 'meditate';
let session = null; // active meditation/breath timer state
let malaCount = 0;

export function render(view) {
  view.innerHTML = `
    <div class="header"><div class="brand">${icon('mind')} Mind Studio</div></div>
    <div class="chips" id="mtabs">
      ${[['meditate', '🧘 Meditate'], ['breathe', '🌬️ Breathe'], ['sleep', '🌙 Sleep Sounds'], ['sos', '🆘 SOS Calm'], ['mantra', '🕉️ Mantra']]
        .map(([id, l]) => `<button class="chip ${tab === id ? 'on' : ''}" data-t="${id}">${l}</button>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div id="mtab"></div>
  `;
  view.querySelector('#mtabs').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const b = e.target.closest('[data-t]');
    if (!b) return;
    stopSession();
    tab = b.dataset.t;
    view.querySelectorAll('#mtabs .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.toggle('on', c === b));
    drawTab(view);
  });
  drawTab(view);
}

function drawTab(view) {
  const box = view.querySelector('#mtab');
  box.innerHTML = '';
  if (tab === 'meditate') drawMeditate(box);
  if (tab === 'breathe') drawBreathe(box);
  if (tab === 'sleep') drawSleep(box);
  if (tab === 'sos') drawSOS(box);
  if (tab === 'mantra') drawMantra(box);
}

/* ---------------- Meditate ---------------- */
function drawMeditate(box) {
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div id="med-player" class="card" style="display:none">
      <div class="center">
        <div class="breath-stage"><div class="breath-circle" id="med-circle">Ready</div></div>
        <div class="big" id="med-name"></div>
        <div class="small muted" id="med-sound"></div>
        <div class="ring mt-14" style="width:150px;height:150px">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 120 120"><circle class="track" cx="60" cy="60" r="52"/>
          <circle class="prog" id="med-prog" cx="60" cy="60" r="52" stroke-dasharray="326.7" stroke-dashoffset="326.7"/></svg>
          <div class="time"><b id="med-time"></b></div>
        </div>
        <div class="small muted mt-8" id="med-line"></div>
        <button class="btn danger wide mt-14" id="med-stop">End session</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="stack mt-14" id="med-list">
      ${MEDITATIONS.map(m => `
        <div class="card tap" data-med="${m.id}">
          <div class="row">
            <div class="e" style="font-size:30px">${m.e}</div>
            <div><div style="font-weight:800">${m.name}</div><div class="small muted">${m.mins} min · ${SOUNDS.find(s => s.id === m.sound)?.name}</div></div>
            <span class="go" style="margin-left:auto">${icon('play')}</span>
          </div>
        </div>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="card mt-14" style="border-color:rgba(157,123,255,.35)">
      <div class="card-title">Gentle movement for heavy days</div>
      <div class="small muted mb-8">When depression makes everything heavy, start tiny. These take under 10 minutes.</div>
      <button class="btn wide" id="med-move">🚶 Try the Heavy Day Lifter</button>
    </div>
    <div class="small muted center mt-14">BiggerBody supports your wellbeing, but it is not medical care. If you are struggling, please reach out to someone you trust or a local professional. You matter. 💛</div>
  `;

  box.querySelector('#med-list').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const card = e.target.closest('[data-med]');
    if (card) startMeditation(MEDITATIONS.find(m => m.id === card.dataset.med));
  });
  box.querySelector('#med-move').addEventListener('click', () => {
    playWorkout('Heavy Day Lifter', bySection('mind'));
  });
  box.querySelector('#med-stop').addEventListener('click', () => stopSession());
}

function startMeditation(m) {
  stopSession();
  const player = document.querySelector('#med-player');
  const list = document.querySelector('#med-list');
  if (!player) return;
  player.style.display = 'block';
  list.style.display = 'none';
  document.querySelector('#med-name').textContent = `${m.e} ${m.name}`;
  document.querySelector('#med-sound').textContent = `Sound: ${SOUNDS.find(s => s.id === m.sound)?.name}`;
  playAmbient(m.sound, 0.5);

  const total = m.mins * 60;
  let left = total, step = 0;
  const circle = document.querySelector('#med-circle');
  const lineEl = document.querySelector('#med-line');
  const timeEl = document.querySelector('#med-time');
  const prog = document.querySelector('#med-prog');
  lineEl.textContent = m.script[0];

  let phaseT = 0;
  session = setInterval(() => {
    left--;
    timeEl.textContent = `${Math.floor(left / 60)}:${String(left % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    prog.style.strokeDashoffset = `${326.7 * (left / total)}`;
    // breathing guide: 4s in, 4s out
    phaseT = (phaseT + 1) % 8;
    if (phaseT < 4) { circle.classList.add('grow'); circle.classList.remove('shrink'); circle.textContent = 'Breathe in'; }
    else { circle.classList.add('shrink'); circle.classList.remove('grow'); circle.textContent = 'Breathe out'; }
    const stepIdx = Math.min(m.script.length - 1, Math.floor(((total - left) / total) * m.script.length));
    if (stepIdx !== step) { step = stepIdx; lineEl.textContent = m.script[step]; beep(740, .1, .08); }
    if (left <= 0) {
      chime();
      addSession('mind', m.mins, 0);
      bumpStat('meditations');
      addXP(30);
      toast('Meditation complete · +30 XP 🧘');
      stopSession();
    }
  }, 1000);
  session.left = left;
}

export function stopSession() {
  if (session) { clearInterval(session); session = null; }
  stopAmbient();
  const player = document.querySelector('#med-player');
  const list = document.querySelector('#med-list');
  if (player) player.style.display = 'none';
  if (list) list.style.display = '';
}

/* ---------------- Breathe ---------------- */
function drawBreathe(box) {
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div class="stack" id="bp-list">
      ${BREATH_PATTERNS.map(p => `
        <div class="card tap" data-bp="${p.id}">
          <div style="font-weight:800">${p.name}</div>
          <div class="small muted mt-8">${p.d}</div>
          <div class="small muted">${p.phases.map(x => x.join('s ')).join(' · ')}</div>
        </div>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div id="bp-player" class="card mt-14" style="display:none">
      <div class="breath-stage"><div class="breath-circle" id="bp-circle">Ready</div></div>
      <div class="center big" id="bp-name"></div>
      <div class="center small muted" id="bp-count">cycles: 0</div>
      <button class="btn danger wide mt-14" id="bp-stop">Stop</button>
    </div>
  `;
  box.querySelector('#bp-list').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const card = e.target.closest('[data-bp]');
    if (!card) return;
    const p = BREATH_PATTERNS.find(x => x.id === card.dataset.bp);
    startBreath(p);
  });
  box.querySelector('#bp-stop')?.addEventListener('click', () => stopSession());
}

function startBreath(p) {
  stopSession();
  document.querySelector('#bp-list').style.display = 'none';
  const player = document.querySelector('#bp-player');
  player.style.display = 'block';
  document.querySelector('#bp-name').textContent = p.name;
  const circle = document.querySelector('#bp-circle');
  const countEl = document.querySelector('#bp-count');
  let cycles = 0, pi = 0, left = p.phases[0][1];
  const setUI = () => {
    const [label, secs] = p.phases[pi];
    circle.textContent = `${label} · ${left}`;
    circle.classList.toggle('grow', label.includes('in'));
    circle.classList.toggle('shrink', label.includes('out'));
  };
  setUI();
  session = setInterval(() => {
    left--;
    if (left <= 0) {
      pi = (pi + 1) % p.phases.length;
      if (pi === 0) { cycles++; countEl.textContent = `cycles: ${cycles}`; beep(660, .08, .08); }
      left = p.phases[pi][1];
      beep(pi === 0 ? 740 : 520, .1, .1);
    }
    setUI();
  }, 1000);
}

/* ---------------- Sleep ---------------- */
function drawSleep(box) {
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div class="stack" id="sound-list">
      ${SOUNDS.map(s => `
        <div class="sound-btn" data-sound="${s.id}">
          <div class="e">${s.e}</div>
          <div><div class="t">${s.name}</div><div class="d">${s.d}</div></div>
          <div class="eq"><i></i><i></i><i></i></div>
        </div>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="card mt-14">
      <div class="card-title">Sleep timer</div>
      <div class="chips" id="sleep-timer">
        ${[15, 30, 60].map(m => `<button class="chip" data-min="${m}">${m} min</button>`).join('')}
        <button class="chip on" data-min="0">No limit</button>
      </div>
      <div class="small muted mt-8">Sound fades out gently when the timer ends.</div>
    </div>
    <div class="small muted center mt-14">Every sound is generated live on your device — infinite, free and offline. 🎧</div>
  `;
  let sleepTimeout = null;
  box.querySelector('#sound-list').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const btn = e.target.closest('[data-sound]');
    if (!btn) return;
    const id = btn.dataset.sound;
    const wasOn = btn.classList.contains('on');
    box.querySelectorAll('.sound-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('on'));
    stopAmbient();
    if (!wasOn) {
      btn.classList.add('on');
      playAmbient(id, 0.55);
    }
  });
  box.querySelector('#sleep-timer').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const b = e.target.closest('[data-min]');
    if (!b) return;
    box.querySelectorAll('#sleep-timer .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.toggle('on', c === b));
    clearTimeout(sleepTimeout);
    const min = +b.dataset.min;
    if (min > 0) {
      sleepTimeout = setTimeout(() => { stopAmbient(); toast('Sleep timer ended — sweet dreams 🌙'); }, min * 60000);
      toast(`Sound stops in ${min} minutes`);
    }
  });
}

/* ---------------- SOS ---------------- */
function drawSOS(box) {
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div class="card" style="border-color:rgba(157,123,255,.4)">
      <div style="font-weight:800;font-size:17px">🆘 Panic or anxiety right now?</div>
      <div class="small muted mt-8">You are safe. Follow the steps below slowly. Nothing else matters for the next minute.</div>
      <button class="btn primary wide mt-14" id="sos-start">Start 60-second calm</button>
    </div>
    <div class="card mt-14">
      <div class="card-title">The 5-4-3-2-1 grounding</div>
      ${SOS_STEPS.map((s, i) => `<div class="row mt-8" style="align-items:flex-start"><b style="color:var(--mind-2)">${i + 1}.</b><span class="small">${s}</span></div>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="card mt-14" style="border-color:rgba(255,154,107,.35)">
      <div class="card-title">For heavy days 💛</div>
      <div class="small">Low mood lies to you. It says "stay down, stay alone." Movement — even 2 minutes — is the kindest counterargument. Try the Heavy Day Lifter above, text a friend, or step into sunlight for 5 minutes.</div>
    </div>
    <div id="sos-player" class="card mt-14" style="display:none">
      <div class="breath-stage"><div class="breath-circle" id="sos-circle">Ready</div></div>
      <div class="center big" id="sos-step"></div>
      <div class="center small muted" id="sos-timer"></div>
      <button class="btn danger wide mt-14" id="sos-stop">I'm okay — stop</button>
    </div>
  `;
  box.querySelector('#sos-start').addEventListener('click', () => {
    stopSession();
    document.querySelector('#sos-player').style.display = 'block';
    playAmbient('ocean', 0.4);
    let step = 0, left = 8;
    const stepEl = document.querySelector('#sos-step');
    const timerEl = document.querySelector('#sos-timer');
    const circle = document.querySelector('#sos-circle');
    const show = () => {
      stepEl.textContent = SOS_STEPS[step] || 'Breathe with the circle.';
      circle.textContent = 'Breathe';
    };
    show();
    session = setInterval(() => {
      left--;
      timerEl.textContent = `${left}s`;
      circle.classList.toggle('grow', left % 8 < 4);
      circle.classList.toggle('shrink', left % 8 >= 4);
      if (left <= 0) {
        step++;
        left = 8;
        beep(600, .12, .1);
        if (step >= SOS_STEPS.length) {
          chime();
          toast('Well done. You handled that. 💛');
          stopSession();
          return;
        }
        show();
      }
    }, 1000);
  });
  box.querySelector('#sos-stop').addEventListener('click', () => stopSession());
}

/* ---------------- Mantra ---------------- */
function drawMantra(box) {
  const done = isMantraDone();
  const CIRC = 2 * Math.PI * 60; // radius 60
  box.innerHTML = `
    <style>
      .mantra-sanskrit { font-size: 34px; line-height: 1.75; text-align: center; margin: 16px 0; font-family: serif; font-weight: 700; }
      .mala-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(12, 1fr); gap: 7px; margin: 14px 0; }
      .mala-bead { width: 30px; height: 30px; border-radius: 50%; border: 2px solid #cbd5e1; background: #f1f5f9; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-size: 10px; transition: all .15s; }
      .mala-bead.on { background: #4f46e5; border-color: #4f46e5; color: #fff; }
      .mala-bead:active { transform: scale(0.9); }
      .mala-prog-wrap { position: relative; width: 150px; height: 150px; margin: 0 auto; }
      .mala-prog-svg { transform: rotate(-90deg); }
      .mala-prog-text { position: absolute; top: 50%; left: 50%; transform: translate(-50%, -50%); font-size: 28px; font-weight: 900; }
    </style>
    <div class="card" style="border-color:rgba(255,154,107,.35)">
      <div class="card-title">Morning Mantra — हस्तको दर्शनम्</div>
      <div class="mantra-sanskrit">
        कराग्रे वसते लक्ष्मीः करमध्ये सरस्वती।<br>
        कराउपस्थे तु गोविन्दः प्रभाते करदर्शनम्।।
      </div>
      <div class="small muted center mt-8">Karāgre Vasate Lakṣmīḥ · Karamadhyhe Sarasvatī · Karāupasthite Tu Govindaḥ · Prabhāte Karadarśanam</div>
      <div class="center mt-14">
        <button class="btn ${done ? 'secondary' : 'primary'} wide" id="mantra-done">${done ? 'Marked done today ✅' : 'I chanted this morning'}</button>
      </div>
      <div class="small muted center mt-8">${done ? 'May your day be blessed 🙏' : 'Traditionally chanted upon waking, looking at your palms.'}</div>
    </div>
    
    <div class="card mt-14" style="border-color:rgba(157,123,255,.35)" id="mala-card">
      <div class="row between" style="align-items:center; margin-bottom:10px">
        <div>
          <div style="font-weight:800">Japa Mala — 108</div>
          <div class="small muted">Om Namaḥ Śivāya · Tap anywhere to chant</div>
        </div>
        <div class="big" id="mala-count" style="font-size:32px;font-weight:900">${malaCount}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="mala-prog-wrap">
        <svg class="mala-prog-svg" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
          <circle class="track" cx="60" cy="60" r="52" fill="none" stroke="#e2e8f0" stroke-width="8"/>
          <circle class="prog" id="mala-prog" cx="60" cy="60" r="52" fill="none" stroke="#4f46e5" stroke-width="8"
                  stroke-dasharray="${CIRC}" stroke-dashoffset="${CIRC}"/>
        </svg>
        <div class="mala-prog-text" id="mala-prog-text">0</div>
      </div>
      <div class="mala-grid" id="mala-grid"></div>
      <div class="center mt-12">
        <button class="btn primary wide" id="mala-tap">Tap anywhere to chant</button>
        <button class="btn secondary wide mt-8" id="mala-reset">Reset Mala</button>
        <button class="btn ${malaCount >= 108 ? 'primary' : 'secondary'} wide mt-8" id="mala-save">${malaCount >= 108 ? 'Save 108 Session' : 'Save Session'}</button>
      </div>
      <div id="mala-msg" class="center small mt-8" style="color:var(--mind-2,#7c3aed);font-weight:700">${malaCount >= 108 ? 'Mala complete! 🕉️' : ''}</div>
    </div>
  `;

  renderMalaGrid();
  updateMalaUI();

  // Click anywhere on the mala card to chant (except on buttons)
  box.querySelector('#mala-card').addEventListener('click', e => {
    if (e.target.closest('button')) return;
    incrementMala();
  });

  // The "Tap anywhere" button also triggers increment (and stops propagation)
  box.querySelector('#mala-tap').addEventListener('click', e => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    incrementMala();
  });

  box.querySelector('#mantra-done')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    setMantraDone(true);
    toast('Mantra marked complete for today 🕉️');
    drawMantra(box);
  });

  box.querySelector('#mala-reset')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    malaCount = 0;
    renderMalaGrid();
    updateMalaUI();
  });

  box.querySelector('#mala-save')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
    if (malaCount === 0) { toast('Nothing to save yet'); return; }
    addChantSession(malaCount, 'Om Namaḥ Śivāya');
    toast(`Saved ${malaCount} chants`);
    malaCount = 0;
    renderMalaGrid();
    updateMalaUI();
  });
}

function incrementMala() {
  if (malaCount >= 108) return;
  malaCount++;
  beep(600, 0.05, 0.1);
  renderMalaGrid();
  updateMalaUI();
  if (malaCount >= 108) {
    chime();
    toast('108 chants complete! 🕉️');
  }
}

function renderMalaGrid() {
  const grid = document.querySelector('#mala-grid');
  if (!grid) return;
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < 108; i++) {
    const b = el(`<div class="mala-bead${i < malaCount ? ' on' : ''}" data-i="${i}"></div>`);
    grid.appendChild(b);
  }
}

function updateMalaUI() {
  const countEl = document.querySelector('#mala-count');
  const progEl = document.querySelector('#mala-prog');
  const progText = document.querySelector('#mala-prog-text');
  const msgEl = document.querySelector('#mala-msg');
  const saveBtn = document.querySelector('#mala-save');
  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = malaCount;
  if (progEl) {
    const offset = CIRC * (1 - malaCount / 108);
    progEl.style.strokeDashoffset = offset;
  }
  if (progText) progText.textContent = malaCount;
  if (msgEl) msgEl.textContent = malaCount >= 108 ? 'Mala complete! 🕉️' : '';
  if (saveBtn) {
    saveBtn.classList.toggle('primary', malaCount >= 108);
    saveBtn.classList.toggle('secondary', malaCount < 108);
  }
}
```

```
// Mind Studio — meditation, breathwork, sleep sounds, SOS calm.
import { el, icon, toast } from '../ui/dom.js';
import { MEDITATIONS, SOUNDS, BREATH_PATTERNS, SOS_STEPS } from '../data/content.js';
import { playAmbient, stopAmbient, chime, beep } from '../modules/audio.js';
import { addSession, addXP, bumpStat, getSettings, isMantraDone, setMantraDone, addChantSession } from '../modules/storage.js';
import { playWorkout } from '../modules/player.js';
import { bySection } from '../data/exercises.js';

let tab = 'meditate';
let session = null; // active meditation/breath timer state
let malaCount = 0;
const CIRC = 2 * Math.PI * 60; // radius 60 — module scope so updateMalaUI can access it

export function render(view) {
  view.innerHTML = `
    <div class="header"><div class="brand">${icon('mind')} Mind Studio</div></div>
    <div class="chips" id="mtabs">
      ${[['meditate', '🧘 Meditate'], ['breathe', '🌬️ Breathe'], ['sleep', '🌙 Sleep Sounds'], ['sos', '🆘 SOS Calm'], ['mantra', '🕉️ Mantra']]
        .map(([id, l]) => `<button class="chip ${tab === id ? 'on' : ''}" data-t="${id}">${l}</button>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div id="mtab"></div>
  `;
  view.querySelector('#mtabs').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const b = e.target.closest('[data-t]');
    if (!b) return;
    stopSession();
    tab = b.dataset.t;
    view.querySelectorAll('#mtabs .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.toggle('on', c === b));
    drawTab(view);
  });
  drawTab(view);
}

function drawTab(view) {
  const box = view.querySelector('#mtab');
  box.innerHTML = '';
  if (tab === 'meditate') drawMeditate(box);
  if (tab === 'breathe') drawBreathe(box);
  if (tab === 'sleep') drawSleep(box);
  if (tab === 'sos') drawSOS(box);
  if (tab === 'mantra') drawMantra(box);
}

/* ---------------- Meditate ---------------- */
function drawMeditate(box) {
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div id="med-player" class="card" style="display:none">
      <div class="center">
        <div class="breath-stage"><div class="breath-circle" id="med-circle">Ready</div></div>
        <div class="big" id="med-name"></div>
        <div class="small muted" id="med-sound"></div>
        <div class="ring mt-14" style="width:150px;height:150px">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 120 120"><circle class="track" cx="60" cy="60" r="52"/>
          <circle class="prog" id="med-prog" cx="60" cy="60" r="52" stroke-dasharray="326.7" stroke-dashoffset="326.7"/></svg>
          <div class="time"><b id="med-time"></b></div>
        </div>
        <div class="small muted mt-8" id="med-line"></div>
        <button class="btn danger wide mt-14" id="med-stop">End session</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="stack mt-14" id="med-list">
      ${MEDITATIONS.map(m => `
        <div class="card tap" data-med="${m.id}">
          <div class="row">
            <div class="e" style="font-size:30px">${m.e}</div>
            <div><div style="font-weight:800">${m.name}</div><div class="small muted">${m.mins} min · ${SOUNDS.find(s => s.id === m.sound)?.name}</div></div>
            <span class="go" style="margin-left:auto">${icon('play')}</span>
          </div>
        </div>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="card mt-14" style="border-color:rgba(157,123,255,.35)">
      <div class="card-title">Gentle movement for heavy days</div>
      <div class="small muted mb-8">When depression makes everything heavy, start tiny. These take under 10 minutes.</div>
      <button class="btn wide" id="med-move">🚶 Try the Heavy Day Lifter</button>
    </div>
    <div class="small muted center mt-14">BiggerBody supports your wellbeing, but it is not medical care. If you are struggling, please reach out to someone you trust or a local professional. You matter. 💛</div>
  `;

  box.querySelector('#med-list').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const card = e.target.closest('[data-med]');
    if (card) startMeditation(MEDITATIONS.find(m => m.id === card.dataset.med));
  });
  box.querySelector('#med-move').addEventListener('click', () => {
    playWorkout('Heavy Day Lifter', bySection('mind'));
  });
  box.querySelector('#med-stop').addEventListener('click', () => stopSession());
}

function startMeditation(m) {
  stopSession();
  const player = document.querySelector('#med-player');
  const list = document.querySelector('#med-list');
  if (!player) return;
  player.style.display = 'block';
  list.style.display = 'none';
  document.querySelector('#med-name').textContent = `${m.e} ${m.name}`;
  document.querySelector('#med-sound').textContent = `Sound: ${SOUNDS.find(s => s.id === m.sound)?.name}`;
  playAmbient(m.sound, 0.5);

  const total = m.mins * 60;
  let left = total, step = 0;
  const circle = document.querySelector('#med-circle');
  const lineEl = document.querySelector('#med-line');
  const timeEl = document.querySelector('#med-time');
  const prog = document.querySelector('#med-prog');
  lineEl.textContent = m.script[0];

  let phaseT = 0;
  session = setInterval(() => {
    left--;
    timeEl.textContent = `${Math.floor(left / 60)}:${String(left % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    prog.style.strokeDashoffset = `${326.7 * (left / total)}`;
    // breathing guide: 4s in, 4s out
    phaseT = (phaseT + 1) % 8;
    if (phaseT < 4) { circle.classList.add('grow'); circle.classList.remove('shrink'); circle.textContent = 'Breathe in'; }
    else { circle.classList.add('shrink'); circle.classList.remove('grow'); circle.textContent = 'Breathe out'; }
    const stepIdx = Math.min(m.script.length - 1, Math.floor(((total - left) / total) * m.script.length));
    if (stepIdx !== step) { step = stepIdx; lineEl.textContent = m.script[step]; beep(740, .1, .08); }
    if (left <= 0) {
      chime();
      addSession('mind', m.mins, 0);
      bumpStat('meditations');
      addXP(30);
      toast('Meditation complete · +30 XP 🧘');
      stopSession();
    }
  }, 1000);
  session.left = left;
}

export function stopSession() {
  if (session) { clearInterval(session); session = null; }
  stopAmbient();
  const player = document.querySelector('#med-player');
  const list = document.querySelector('#med-list');
  if (player) player.style.display = 'none';
  if (list) list.style.display = '';
}

/* ---------------- Breathe ---------------- */
function drawBreathe(box) {
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div class="stack" id="bp-list">
      ${BREATH_PATTERNS.map(p => `
        <div class="card tap" data-bp="${p.id}">
          <div style="font-weight:800">${p.name}</div>
          <div class="small muted mt-8">${p.d}</div>
          <div class="small muted">${p.phases.map(x => x.join('s ')).join(' · ')}</div>
        </div>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div id="bp-player" class="card mt-14" style="display:none">
      <div class="breath-stage"><div class="breath-circle" id="bp-circle">Ready</div></div>
      <div class="center big" id="bp-name"></div>
      <div class="center small muted" id="bp-count">cycles: 0</div>
      <button class="btn danger wide mt-14" id="bp-stop">Stop</button>
    </div>
  `;
  box.querySelector('#bp-list').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const card = e.target.closest('[data-bp]');
    if (!card) return;
    const p = BREATH_PATTERNS.find(x => x.id === card.dataset.bp);
    startBreath(p);
  });
  box.querySelector('#bp-stop')?.addEventListener('click', () => stopSession());
}

function startBreath(p) {
  stopSession();
  document.querySelector('#bp-list').style.display = 'none';
  const player = document.querySelector('#bp-player');
  player.style.display = 'block';
  document.querySelector('#bp-name').textContent = p.name;
  const circle = document.querySelector('#bp-circle');
  const countEl = document.querySelector('#bp-count');
  let cycles = 0, pi = 0, left = p.phases[0][1];
  const setUI = () => {
    const [label, secs] = p.phases[pi];
    circle.textContent = `${label} · ${left}`;
    circle.classList.toggle('grow', label.includes('in'));
    circle.classList.toggle('shrink', label.includes('out'));
  };
  setUI();
  session = setInterval(() => {
    left--;
    if (left <= 0) {
      pi = (pi + 1) % p.phases.length;
      if (pi === 0) { cycles++; countEl.textContent = `cycles: ${cycles}`; beep(660, .08, .08); }
      left = p.phases[pi][1];
      beep(pi === 0 ? 740 : 520, .1, .1);
    }
    setUI();
  }, 1000);
}

/* ---------------- Sleep ---------------- */
function drawSleep(box) {
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div class="stack" id="sound-list">
      ${SOUNDS.map(s => `
        <div class="sound-btn" data-sound="${s.id}">
          <div class="e">${s.e}</div>
          <div><div class="t">${s.name}</div><div class="d">${s.d}</div></div>
          <div class="eq"><i></i><i></i><i></i></div>
        </div>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="card mt-14">
      <div class="card-title">Sleep timer</div>
      <div class="chips" id="sleep-timer">
        ${[15, 30, 60].map(m => `<button class="chip" data-min="${m}">${m} min</button>`).join('')}
        <button class="chip on" data-min="0">No limit</button>
      </div>
      <div class="small muted mt-8">Sound fades out gently when the timer ends.</div>
    </div>
    <div class="small muted center mt-14">Every sound is generated live on your device — infinite, free and offline. 🎧</div>
  `;
  let sleepTimeout = null;
  box.querySelector('#sound-list').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const btn = e.target.closest('[data-sound]');
    if (!btn) return;
    const id = btn.dataset.sound;
    const wasOn = btn.classList.contains('on');
    box.querySelectorAll('.sound-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('on'));
    stopAmbient();
    if (!wasOn) {
      btn.classList.add('on');
      playAmbient(id, 0.55);
    }
  });
  box.querySelector('#sleep-timer').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const b = e.target.closest('[data-min]');
    if (!b) return;
    box.querySelectorAll('#sleep-timer .chip').forEach(c => c.classList.toggle('on', c === b));
    clearTimeout(sleepTimeout);
    const min = +b.dataset.min;
    if (min > 0) {
      sleepTimeout = setTimeout(() => { stopAmbient(); toast('Sleep timer ended — sweet dreams 🌙'); }, min * 60000);
      toast(`Sound stops in ${min} minutes`);
    }
  });
}

/* ---------------- SOS ---------------- */
function drawSOS(box) {
  box.innerHTML = `
    <div class="card" style="border-color:rgba(157,123,255,.4)">
      <div style="font-weight:800;font-size:17px">🆘 Panic or anxiety right now?</div>
      <div class="small muted mt-8">You are safe. Follow the steps below slowly. Nothing else matters for the next minute.</div>
      <button class="btn primary wide mt-14" id="sos-start">Start 60-second calm</button>
    </div>
    <div class="card mt-14">
      <div class="card-title">The 5-4-3-2-1 grounding</div>
      ${SOS_STEPS.map((s, i) => `<div class="row mt-8" style="align-items:flex-start"><b style="color:var(--mind-2)">${i + 1}.</b><span class="small">${s}</span></div>`).join('')}
    </div>
    <div class="card mt-14" style="border-color:rgba(255,154,107,.35)">
      <div class="card-title">For heavy days 💛</div>
      <div class="small">Low mood lies to you. It says "stay down, stay alone." Movement — even 2 minutes — is the kindest counterargument. Try the Heavy Day Lifter above, text a friend, or step into sunlight for 5 minutes.</div>
    </div>
    <div id="sos-player" class="card mt-14" style="display:none">
      <div class="breath-stage"><div class="breath-circle" id="sos-circle">Ready</div></div>
      <div class="center big" id="sos-step"></div>
      <div class="center small muted" id="sos-timer"></div>
      <button class="btn danger wide mt-14" id="sos-stop">I'm okay — stop</button>
    </div>
  `;
  box.querySelector('#sos-start').addEventListener('click', () => {
    stopSession();
    document.querySelector('#sos-player').style.display = 'block';
    playAmbient('ocean', 0.4);
    let step = 0, left = 8;
    const stepEl = document.querySelector('#sos-step');
    const timerEl = document.querySelector('#sos-timer');
    const circle = document.querySelector('#sos-circle');
    const show = () => {
      stepEl.textContent = SOS_STEPS[step] || 'Breathe with the circle.';
      circle.textContent = 'Breathe';
    };
    show();
    session = setInterval(() => {
      left--;
      timerEl.textContent = `${left}s`;
      circle.classList.toggle('grow', left % 8 < 4);
      circle.classList.toggle('shrink', left % 8 >= 4);
      if (left <= 0) {
        step++;
        left = 8;
        beep(600, .12, .1);
        if (step >= SOS_STEPS.length) {
          chime();
          toast('Well done. You handled that. 💛');
          stopSession();
          return;
        }
        show();
      }
    }, 1000);
  });
  box.querySelector('#sos-stop').addEventListener('click', () => stopSession());
}

/* ---------------- Mantra ---------------- */
function drawMantra(box) {
  const done = isMantraDone();
  box.innerHTML = `
    <style>
      .mantra-sanskrit { font-size: 34px; line-height: 1.75; text-align: center; margin: 16px 0; font-family: serif; font-weight: 700; }
      .mala-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(12, 1fr); gap: 7px; margin: 14px 0; }
      .mala-bead { width: 30px; height: 30px; border-radius: 50%; border: 2px solid #cbd5e1; background: #f1f5f9; cursor: pointer; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; font-size: 10px; transition: all .15s; }
      .mala-bead.on { background: #4f46e5; border-color: #4f46e5; color: #fff; }
      .mala-bead:active { transform: scale(0.9); }
      .mala-prog-wrap { position: relative; width: 150px; height: 150px; margin: 0 auto; }
      .mala-prog-svg { transform: rotate(-90deg); }
      .mala-prog-text { position: absolute; top: 50%; left: 50%; transform: translate(-50%, -50%); font-size: 28px; font-weight: 900; }
    </style>
    <div class="card" style="border-color:rgba(255,154,107,.35)">
      <div class="card-title">Morning Mantra — हस्तको दर्शनम्</div>
      <div class="mantra-sanskrit">
        कराग्रे वसते लक्ष्मीः करमध्ये सरस्वती।<br>
        कराउपस्थे तु गोविन्दः प्रभाते करदर्शनम्।।
      </div>
      <div class="small muted center mt-8">Karāgre Vasate Lakṣmīḥ · Karamadhyhe Sarasvatī · Karāupasthite Tu Govindaḥ · Prabhāte Karadarśanam</div>
      <div class="center mt-14">
        <button class="btn ${done ? 'secondary' : 'primary'} wide" id="mantra-done">${done ? 'Marked done today ✅' : 'I chanted this morning'}</button>
      </div>
      <div class="small muted center mt-8">${done ? 'May your day be blessed 🙏' : 'Traditionally chanted upon waking, looking at your palms.'}</div>
    </div>
    
    <div class="card mt-14" style="border-color:rgba(157,123,255,.35)" id="mala-card">
      <div class="row between" style="align-items:center; margin-bottom:10px">
        <div>
          <div style="font-weight:800">Japa Mala — 108</div>
          <div class="small muted">Om Namaḥ Śivāya · Tap anywhere to chant</div>
        </div>
        <div class="big" id="mala-count" style="font-size:32px;font-weight:900">${malaCount}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="mala-prog-wrap">
        <svg class="mala-prog-svg" viewBox="0 0 120 120">
          <circle class="track" cx="60" cy="60" r="52" fill="none" stroke="#e2e8f0" stroke-width="8"/>
          <circle class="prog" id="mala-prog" cx="60" cy="60" r="52" fill="none" stroke="#4f46e5" stroke-width="8"
                  stroke-dasharray="${CIRC}" stroke-dashoffset="${CIRC}"/>
        </svg>
        <div class="mala-prog-text" id="mala-prog-text">0</div>
      </div>
      <div class="mala-grid" id="mala-grid"></div>
      <div class="center mt-12">
        <button class="btn primary wide" id="mala-tap">Tap anywhere to chant</button>
        <button class="btn secondary wide mt-8" id="mala-reset">Reset Mala</button>
        <button class="btn ${malaCount >= 108 ? 'primary' : 'secondary'} wide mt-8" id="mala-save">${malaCount >= 108 ? 'Save 108 Session' : 'Save Session'}</button>
      </div>
      <div id="mala-msg" class="center small mt-8" style="color:var(--mind-2,#7c3aed);font-weight:700">${malaCount >= 108 ? 'Mala complete! 🕉️' : ''}</div>
    </div>
  `;

  renderMalaGrid();
  updateMalaUI();

  // Click anywhere on the mala card to chant (except on buttons)
  const malaCard = box.querySelector('#mala-card');
  if (malaCard) {
    malaCard.addEventListener('click', e => {
      // Ignore clicks on buttons and their children
      if (e.target.closest('button')) return;
      incrementMala();
    });
  }

  // The "Tap anywhere" button also triggers increment
  const tapBtn = box.querySelector('#mala-tap');
  if (tapBtn) {
    tapBtn.addEventListener('click', e => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      incrementMala();
    });
  }

  const doneBtn = box.querySelector('#mantra-done');
  if (doneBtn) {
    doneBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      setMantraDone(true);
      toast('Mantra marked complete for today 🕉️');
      drawMantra(box);
    });
  }

  const resetBtn = box.querySelector('#mala-reset');
  if (resetBtn) {
    resetBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      malaCount = 0;
      renderMalaGrid();
      updateMalaUI();
    });
  }

  const saveBtn = box.querySelector('#mala-save');
  if (saveBtn) {
    saveBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (malaCount === 0) { toast('Nothing to save yet'); return; }
      addChantSession(malaCount, 'Om Namaḥ Śivāya');
      toast(`Saved ${malaCount} chants`);
      malaCount = 0;
      renderMalaGrid();
      updateMalaUI();
    });
  }
}

function incrementMala() {
  if (malaCount >= 108) return;
  malaCount++;
  beep(600, 0.05, 0.1);
  renderMalaGrid();
  updateMalaUI();
  if (malaCount >= 108) {
    chime();
    toast('108 chants complete! 🕉️');
  }
}

function renderMalaGrid() {
  const grid = document.querySelector('#mala-grid');
  if (!grid) return;
  grid.innerHTML = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < 108; i++) {
    const b = el(`<div class="mala-bead${i < malaCount ? ' on' : ''}" data-i="${i}"></div>`);
    grid.appendChild(b);
  }
}

function updateMalaUI() {
  const countEl = document.querySelector('#mala-count');
  const progEl = document.querySelector('#mala-prog');
  const progText = document.querySelector('#mala-prog-text');
  const msgEl = document.querySelector('#mala-msg');
  const saveBtn = document.querySelector('#mala-save');
  if (countEl) countEl.textContent = malaCount;
  if (progEl) {
    const offset = CIRC * (1 - malaCount / 108);
    progEl.style.strokeDashoffset = offset;
  }
  if (progText) progText.textContent = malaCount;
  if (msgEl) msgEl.textContent = malaCount >= 108 ? 'Mala complete! 🕉️' : '';
  if (saveBtn) {
    saveBtn.classList.toggle('primary', malaCount >= 108);
    saveBtn.classList.toggle('secondary', malaCount < 108);
  }
}
```
